<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: center"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>BAYVIEW
ACQUISITION CORP</b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: center"><span id="a_007"></span><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>NOTES
TO FINANCIAL STATEMENTS</b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: center"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>MARCH
31, 2026</b>&#160;&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: center"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>(UNAUDITED)</b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: center"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000366" name="us-gaap:OrganizationConsolidationAndPresentationOfFinancialStatementsDisclosureTextBlock"><p id="xdx_809_eus-gaap--OrganizationConsolidationAndPresentationOfFinancialStatementsDisclosureTextBlock_zYyL1F5PNTjl" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>NOTE
1 &#8212;<span id="xdx_82F_z6VD9IAWOPp6">ORGANIZATION AND BUSINESS OPERATIONS</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: center"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><i>Organizational
and General</i></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Bayview
Acquisition Corp (the &#8220;Company&#8221;) was incorporated in the Cayman Islands on February 16, 2023. The Company was formed for
the purpose of effecting a merger, capital stock exchange, asset acquisition, stock purchase, reorganization, or similar business combination
with one or more businesses or entities (the &#8220;Business Combination&#8221;).</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company is not limited to a particular industry or sector for purposes of consummating a Business Combination. The Company is an early
stage and emerging growth company and, as such, the Company is subject to all of the risks associated with early stage and emerging growth
companies.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company&#8217;s sponsors are Peace Investment Holdings Limited, a British Virgin Islands company, and Bayview Holding LP, a Delaware
limited partnership (the &#8220;Sponsors&#8221;). As of March 31, 2026, the Company had not commenced any operations. All activities
for the period from February 16, 2023 (inception) through March 31, 2026 related to the Company&#8217;s formation and the initial public
offering (&#8220;IPO&#8221;), and subsequent to the IPO, identifying a target company for an initial Business Combination. The Company
will not generate any operating revenues until after the completion of an initial Business Combination, at the earliest. The Company
will generate non-operating income in the form of interest or dividend income from the proceeds derived from the IPO. The Company has
selected December 31 as its fiscal year end.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
registration statement for the Company&#8217;s IPO (the &#8220;Registration Statement&#8221;) was declared effective on December 14,
2023. Additionally, on December 14, 2023, the Company filed a registration statement adding securities to the Registration Statement.
On December 19, 2023, the Company consummated the IPO of <span id="xdx_905_eus-gaap--StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues_c20231219__20231219__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--IPOMember_z15gyUlUnUv8" title="Number of share units issued"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues" contextRef="From2023-12-192023-12-19_us-gaap_IPOMember" id="Fact000368" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">6,000,000</ix:nonFraction></span> units, (&#8220;Units&#8221; and, with respect to the ordinary shares
included in the Units being offered, the &#8220;Public Shares&#8221;) at $<span id="xdx_90D_eus-gaap--SharesIssuedPricePerShare_iI_pid_c20231219__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--IPOMember_zEoLz4xPsNId" title="Share price"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SharesIssuedPricePerShare" contextRef="AsOf2023-12-19_us-gaap_IPOMember" id="Fact000370" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="USDPShares">10.00</ix:nonFraction></span> per Unit, generating gross proceeds of $<span id="xdx_90E_eus-gaap--ProceedsFromIssuanceInitialPublicOffering_c20231219__20231219__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--IPOMember_zTDWm36HTvu6" title="Proceeds from initial public offering"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ProceedsFromIssuanceInitialPublicOffering" contextRef="From2023-12-192023-12-19_us-gaap_IPOMember" id="Fact000372" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">60,000,000</ix:nonFraction></span>, which
is described in Note 3, and the sale of <span id="xdx_90E_eus-gaap--StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues_c20231219__20231219__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--PrivatePlacementMember_zWuMwYDBwyug" title="Number of share units issued"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues" contextRef="From2023-12-192023-12-19_us-gaap_PrivatePlacementMember" id="Fact000374" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">232,500</ix:nonFraction></span> Units (the &#8220;Private Placement Units&#8221;) at a price of $<span id="xdx_90C_eus-gaap--SharesIssuedPricePerShare_iI_pid_c20231219__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--PrivatePlacementMember_zKPVqfB9Zmr5" title="Share price"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SharesIssuedPricePerShare" contextRef="AsOf2023-12-19_us-gaap_PrivatePlacementMember" id="Fact000376" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="USDPShares">10.00</ix:nonFraction></span> per Private Placement
Unit in private placements to the Sponsors that was closed simultaneously with the IPO, generating gross proceeds of $<span id="xdx_90F_eus-gaap--ProceedsFromIssuanceOfPrivatePlacement_c20231219__20231219__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--PrivatePlacementMember_zcW2b2YH3xj5" title="Proceeds from private placement"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ProceedsFromIssuanceOfPrivatePlacement" contextRef="From2023-12-192023-12-19_us-gaap_PrivatePlacementMember" id="Fact000378" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">2,325,000</ix:nonFraction></span>.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company granted the underwriter a 45-day option from the date of the IPO to purchase up to <span id="xdx_906_eus-gaap--SaleOfStockNumberOfSharesIssuedInTransaction_c20231214__20231214__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--OverAllotmentOptionMember_zyWv4UVckiQ5" title="Stock issued during the period, shares"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SaleOfStockNumberOfSharesIssuedInTransaction" contextRef="From2023-12-142023-12-14_us-gaap_OverAllotmentOptionMember" id="Fact000380" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">900,000</ix:nonFraction></span>
additional Units to cover over-allotment, if any, at the IPO price less the underwriting discounts and commissions. On January 28,
2024, the underwriter&#8217;s over-allotment option expired unexercised and hence a total of <span id="xdx_90D_eus-gaap--ShareBasedCompensationArrangementByShareBasedPaymentAwardOptionsForfeituresInPeriod_c20240128__20240128__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--OverAllotmentOptionMember_zvbbpXPcKwq9" title="Number of shares forfeited by sponsors"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ShareBasedCompensationArrangementByShareBasedPaymentAwardOptionsForfeituresInPeriod" contextRef="From2024-01-282024-01-28_us-gaap_OverAllotmentOptionMember" id="Fact000382" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">225,000</ix:nonFraction></span>
ordinary shares were forfeited by the Sponsors.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company will have until the last extended date, June 19, 2026, to consummate a Business Combination. However, if the Company has not
completed a Business Combination within the Combination Period, the Company will (i) cease all operations except for the purpose of winding
up, (ii) as promptly as reasonably possible but not more than ten business days thereafter, redeem <span id="xdx_908_ecustom--PercentageOfOutstandingVotingSecurities_pid_dp_uPure_c20260101__20260331_zNRGZXVqraZd" title="Percentage of outstanding voting securities"><ix:nonFraction name="BAYA:PercentageOfOutstandingVotingSecurities" contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" id="Fact000384" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">100</ix:nonFraction></span>% of the Public Shares, at a per-share
price, payable in cash, equal to the aggregate amount then on deposit in the Trust Account, including interest earned and not previously
released to us to pay our taxes, if any (less certain amount of interest to pay liquidation and dissolution expenses), divided by the
number of then issued and outstanding Public Shares, which redemption will completely extinguish the rights of the Public Shareholders
as shareholders (including the right to receive further liquidating distributions, if any), and (iii) as promptly as reasonably possible
following such redemption, subject to the approval of the Company&#8217;s remaining Public Shareholders and its Board of Directors, liquidate
and dissolve, subject in each case to the Company&#8217;s obligations under Cayman Islands law to provide for claims of creditors and
the requirements of other applicable law.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><i>Proposed
Business Combination</i></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">On
June 7, 2024, the Company entered into the execution of an Agreement and Plan of Merger (the &#8220;Merger Agreement&#8221;), by and
among the Company, Oabay Holding Company, a Cayman Islands exempted company limited by shares (&#8220;PubCo&#8221;), Bayview Acquisition
Corp, a Cayman Islands exempted company limited by shares (&#8220;SPAC&#8221;), Bayview Merger Sub 1 Limited, a Cayman Islands exempted
company limited by shares and a wholly-owned subsidiary of PubCo (&#8220;Merger Sub 1&#8221;), Bayview Merger Sub 2 Limited, a Cayman
Islands exempted company limited by shares and a wholly-owned subsidiary of PubCo (&#8220;Merger Sub 2&#8221;), Oabay Merger Sub Limited,
a Cayman Islands exempted company limited by shares and a wholly-owned subsidiary of PubCo (&#8220;Merger Sub 3&#8221;), BLAFC Limited,
a business company limited by shares in the British Virgin Islands, Bayview Holding LP, a Delaware limited partnership, and Peace Investment
Holdings Limited, pursuant to which, subject to the satisfaction or waiver of certain conditions set forth therein, (i) SPAC will merge
with and into Merger Sub 1, with SPAC surviving the merger in accordance with the Companies Act (As Revised) of the Cayman Islands (the
&#8220;Act&#8221;) (the &#8220;First SPAC Merger&#8221;), (ii) immediately following the First SPAC Merger, SPAC will merge with and
into Merger Sub 2, with Merger Sub 2 surviving the Merger in accordance with the Act (the &#8220;Second SPAC Merger&#8221; and together
with the First SPAC Merger, the &#8220;Initial Mergers&#8221;), and (iii) following the Initial Mergers, Merger Sub 3 will merge with
and into the Company, with the Company being the surviving entity and becoming a wholly-owned subsidiary of PubCo in accordance with
the Act (the transactions contemplated by the Merger Agreement, the &#8220;Business Combination&#8221;).</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">On
June 26, 2024, the Company entered into Amendment No. 1 to the Merger Agreement, pursuant to which all parties agreed to revise the earnout
milestones to reflect new consolidated revenue metrics. Among other things, the <span id="xdx_90F_ecustom--BusinessCombinationRevenueEarnoutDescription_c20240626__20240626__us-gaap--BusinessAcquisitionAxis__us-gaap--SeriesOfIndividuallyImmaterialBusinessAcquisitionsMember_zX1l7GHVP7Ve" title="Revenue earnout description"><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2024-06-262024-06-26_us-gaap_SeriesOfIndividuallyImmaterialBusinessAcquisitionsMember" id="Fact000386" name="BAYA:BusinessCombinationRevenueEarnoutDescription">Amendment revises the Merger Agreement to provide that,
if the PubCo 2024 Audited Financials (as defined in the Merger Agreement) do not reflect consolidated revenue in excess of RMB 436,000,000.00
during fiscal year 2024 or the PubCo 2025 Audited Financials (as defined in the Merger Agreement) do not reflect consolidated revenues
in excess of RMB 583,000,000.00 during fiscal year 2025, but the total consolidated revenue reflected by the PubCo 2024 Audited Financials
and the PubCo 2025 Audited Financials is in excess of RMB 1,019,000,00.00, the Pro Rata Portion (as defined in the Merger Agreement)
of 6,000,000 Earnout Shares (as defined in the Merger Agreement) will be issued and delivered by PubCo to each Earnout Shareholder (as
defined in the Merger Agreement) within five (5) business days following the date of filing of the PubCo 2025 Audited Financials. Previously,
the Pro Rata Portion of 6,000,000 Earnout Shares was to be delivered only if the PubCo 2024 Audited Financials do not reflect consolidated
revenue in excess of RMB 436,000,000.00 during fiscal year 2024 but the total consolidated revenue reflected by the PubCo 2024 Audited
Financials and the PubCo 2025 Audited Financials is in excess of RMB 1,019,000,00.00 during fiscal year 2025.</ix:nonNumeric></span></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">On
May 14, 2025, the parties to the Merger Agreement entered into the Second Amendment to the Agreement and Plan of Merger pursuant to which
the parties agreed to realign the sequence of the transactions contemplated by the Merger Agreement so that such sequence is as follows:
(i) Merger Sub 3 will merge with and into Oabay, with Oabay being the surviving entity and becoming a wholly owned subsidiary of PubCo
(the &#8220;Acquisition Merger&#8221;), and (ii) immediately following the Acquisition Merger, Merger Sub 1 will merge with and into
SPAC, with SPAC being the surviving entity (the &#8220;First SPAC Merger&#8221; and together with the Acquisition Merger, the &#8220;Mergers&#8221;),
each Merger to occur upon the terms and subject to the conditions set forth in the Second Amendment and in accordance with the applicable
provisions of the Companies Act (As Revised) of the Cayman Islands.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">On
January 21, 2026, the parties to the Merger Agreement entered into the Third Amendment to the Agreement and Plan of Merger, which extended
the Outside Closing Date to June 15, 2026.&#160;&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">On May 19, 2026, the parties to the Merger Agreement entered into the
Fourth Amendment to the Agreement and Plan of Merger, which extended the Outside Closing Date to December 19, 2026.</p>
<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><i>Extension</i></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">On
September 16, 2024, the Company held an extraordinary general meeting virtually and in person, solely with respect to voting on (i)
the proposal to extend the date by which the Company must complete its initial business combination from September 19, 2024 to June
19, 2025, with all nine (9) extensions comprised of one month each (the &#8220;2024 Extension Amendment Proposal&#8221;) and (ii)
the proposal to amend the Company&#8217;s investment management trust agreement, dated December 14, 2023 by and between the Company
and Equiniti Trust Company, LLC (the &#8220;Trustee&#8221;) to allow the Company to extend the Termination Date up to nine (9)
times, with all nine (9) extensions comprised of one month each from the Termination Date to June 19, 2025 by providing five
days&#8217; advance notice to the Trustee prior to the applicable Termination Date and depositing into the Trust Account $<span id="xdx_90B_eus-gaap--AssetsHeldInTrust_iI_c20240916_zj9KE1lHyTod" title="Deposit into trust account"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AssetsHeldInTrust" contextRef="AsOf2024-09-16" id="Fact000388" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">125,000</ix:nonFraction></span>
for each month in an Extension until June 19, 2025 (the &#8220;2024 Trust Agreement Amendment Proposal&#8221;).</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">In
connection with the vote to approve the 2024 Extension Amendment Proposal and the 2024 Trust Agreement Amendment Proposal at the
Extraordinary General Meeting on September 16, 2024, the holders of <span id="xdx_906_eus-gaap--StockRedeemedOrCalledDuringPeriodShares_c20240916__20240916_zkqLX0EAKxt4" title="Redeemed ordinary shares"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockRedeemedOrCalledDuringPeriodShares" contextRef="From2024-09-162024-09-16" id="Fact000390" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">2,290,989</ix:nonFraction></span>
Ordinary Shares properly exercised their rights to redeem their shares for cash at a redemption price of approximately $<span id="xdx_904_eus-gaap--TemporaryEquityRedemptionPricePerShare_iI_c20240916_z1WD5a0nlyc4" title="Redemption price"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:TemporaryEquityRedemptionPricePerShare" contextRef="AsOf2024-09-16" id="Fact000392" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="USDPShares">10.39</ix:nonFraction></span>
per share, for an aggregate redemption amount of approximately $<span id="xdx_909_eus-gaap--PaymentsForRepurchaseOfCommonStock_c20240916__20240916_zIoadTkgNfXc" title="Aggregate redemption amount"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:PaymentsForRepurchaseOfCommonStock" contextRef="From2024-09-162024-09-16" id="Fact000394" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">23,803,376</ix:nonFraction></span>.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">On
June 17, 2025, the Company held another extraordinary general meeting virtually and in person, solely with respect to voting on (i) the
proposal to extend the date by which the Company must complete its initial business combination from June 19, 2025 up to six times to
December 19, 2025, with all six extensions comprised of one month each (the &#8220;2025 Extension Amendment Proposal&#8221;) and (ii)
the proposal to amend the Company&#8217;s investment management trust agreement, dated December 14, 2023 by and between the Company and
the Trustee to (a) allow the Company to extend the Termination Date up to six times from the Termination Date to December 19, 2025 with
all six extensions comprised of one month each by providing five days&#8217; advance notice to the Trustee and depositing into the Trust
Account a payment of $<span id="xdx_905_eus-gaap--AssetsHeldInTrust_iI_c20250617_zDL2FgkYCOV1" title="Deposit into trust account"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AssetsHeldInTrust" contextRef="AsOf2025-06-17" id="Fact000396" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">100,000</ix:nonFraction></span> per extension (the &#8220;Extension Payment&#8221;) until December 19, 2025 and (b) allow the Trustee to
liquidate the Trust Account if the Extension Payment is not deposited on time after the expiration of a 30-day cure period (the &#8220;2025
Trust Agreement Amendment Proposal&#8221;).</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">In
connection with the vote to approve the 2025 Extension Amendment Proposal and the 2025 Trust Agreement Amendment Proposal at the Extraordinary
General Meeting on June 17, 2025, the holders of <span id="xdx_908_eus-gaap--StockRedeemedOrCalledDuringPeriodShares_c20250617__20250617_zp8N81cKbZeg" title="Redeemed ordinary shares"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockRedeemedOrCalledDuringPeriodShares" contextRef="From2025-06-172025-06-17" id="Fact000398" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">1,975,249</ix:nonFraction></span> Ordinary Shares properly exercised their rights to redeem their shares for
cash at a redemption price of approximately $<span id="xdx_900_eus-gaap--TemporaryEquityRedemptionPricePerShare_iI_c20250617_zcoGzbBizvF7" title="Redemption price"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:TemporaryEquityRedemptionPricePerShare" contextRef="AsOf2025-06-17" id="Fact000400" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="USDPShares">11.05</ix:nonFraction></span> per share, for an aggregate redemption amount of approximately $<span id="xdx_90E_eus-gaap--PaymentsForRepurchaseOfCommonStock_c20250617__20250617_zNlvVgcSx9Wf" title="Aggregate redemption amount"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:PaymentsForRepurchaseOfCommonStock" contextRef="From2025-06-172025-06-17" id="Fact000402" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">21,826,501</ix:nonFraction></span>.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">On
December 12, 2025, the Company held an extraordinary general meeting virtually and in person, solely with respect to voting on (i) the
proposal to extend the date by which the Company must complete its initial business combination from December 19, 2025 to June 19, 2026,
with all six extensions comprised of one month each (the &#8220;2025 Extension Amendment Proposal 2&#8221;) and (ii) the proposal to
amend the Company&#8217;s investment management trust agreement, dated December 14, 2023 by and between the Company and the Trustee to
allow the Company to extend the Termination Date up to six times from the Termination Date to June 19, 2026 with all six extensions comprised
of one month each by providing five days&#8217; advance notice to the Trustee and depositing into the Trust Account a payment of $<span id="xdx_909_eus-gaap--CommonStockHeldInTrust_iI_c20251212_zZ3y3eAjDLhj" title="Held in trust"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:CommonStockHeldInTrust" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-12" id="Fact000404" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">50,000</ix:nonFraction></span>
per extension until June 19, 2026 (the &#8220;Trust Agreement Amendment Proposal 2&#8221;).</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">In connection with the vote to approve the 2025 Extension Amendment
Proposal 2 and the 2025 Trust Agreement Amendment Proposal 2, the holders of <span id="xdx_905_eus-gaap--StockRedeemedOrCalledDuringPeriodShares_c20251219__20251219_z3oqPVWdhpE8" title="Redeemed ordinary shares"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockRedeemedOrCalledDuringPeriodShares" contextRef="From2025-12-192025-12-19" id="Fact000406" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">727,970</ix:nonFraction></span> Ordinary Shares properly exercised their rights to
redeem their shares for cash at a redemption price of approximately $<span id="xdx_901_eus-gaap--TemporaryEquityRedemptionPricePerShare_iI_c20251219_zcMa7n5bu8R8" title="Redemption price"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:TemporaryEquityRedemptionPricePerShare" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-19" id="Fact000408" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="USDPShares">11.53</ix:nonFraction></span> per share, for an aggregate redemption amount of approximately
$<span id="xdx_90B_eus-gaap--PaymentsForRepurchaseOfCommonStock_pp2d_c20251219__20251219_z6KJelz7NLpd" title="Aggregate redemption amount"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:PaymentsForRepurchaseOfCommonStock" contextRef="From2025-12-192025-12-19" id="Fact000410" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="2" unitRef="USD">8,393,494.10</ix:nonFraction></span>.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">From
September 2024 through March 2026, the Company issued 11 promissory notes to Oabay to cover the expense in connection with the extension
of the Business Combination, for an aggregate principal amount of $<span id="xdx_90B_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentFaceAmount_iI_c20260331__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--ExtensionNoteOneMember_zzacxEES1kt8" title="Outstanding amount"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentFaceAmount" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_custom_ExtensionNoteOneMember" id="Fact000412" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">1,925,000</ix:nonFraction></span></span><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">.
Through March 31, 2026, an aggregate of $<span id="xdx_90C_eus-gaap--ProceedsFromRelatedPartyDebt_c20260301__20260331__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--ExtensionNoteOneMember__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--OabayMember_zFvkqNm4s7Bb" title="Proceeds from related party debt"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ProceedsFromRelatedPartyDebt" contextRef="From2026-03-012026-03-31_custom_ExtensionNoteOneMember_custom_OabayMember" id="Fact000414" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">1,925,000</ix:nonFraction></span> funded by Oabay pursuant to promissory note arrangements was deposited into the trust
account.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><i>Going
Concern Consideration</i></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">As
of March 31, 2026, the Company had cash of $<span id="xdx_905_eus-gaap--Cash_iI_c20260331_zKHZONjyzm6i" title="Cash"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:Cash" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000416" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">50,187</ix:nonFraction></span> and a working capital deficit of $<span id="xdx_901_ecustom--WorkingCapital_iI_c20260331_zYPsVYBIJJH9" title="Working capital"><ix:nonFraction name="BAYA:WorkingCapital" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000418" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">4,035,754</ix:nonFraction></span>. The Company has incurred and expects
to continue to incur significant professional costs to remain as a public traded company and to incur transaction costs in pursuit of
a Business Combination. In connection with the Company&#8217;s assessment of going concern considerations in accordance with Accounting
Standards Update (&#8220;ASU&#8221;) 2014-15, &#8220;Disclosures of Uncertainties about an Entity&#8217;s Ability to Continue as a Going
Concern,&#8221; management believes that these conditions raise substantial doubt about the Company&#8217;s ability to continue as a
going concern. In addition, if the Company is unable to complete a Business Combination within the Combination Period and such period
is not extended, there will be a liquidation and subsequent dissolution. As a result, management has determined that such additional
condition also raises substantial doubt about the Company&#8217;s ability to continue as a going concern. The financial statements do
not include any adjustments that might result from the outcome of the uncertainty.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000420" name="us-gaap:SignificantAccountingPoliciesTextBlock"><p id="xdx_80D_eus-gaap--SignificantAccountingPoliciesTextBlock_zln06ch4kej2" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>NOTE
2 &#8212; <span id="xdx_821_zCrDffUxcAw2">SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000422" name="us-gaap:BasisOfAccountingPolicyPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_844_eus-gaap--BasisOfAccountingPolicyPolicyTextBlock_zZXG5dJQ8Faa" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><i><span id="xdx_86F_zqCMZLsT1Xeg">Basis
of Presentation</span></i></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
accompanying financial statements are presented in conformity with accounting principles generally accepted in the United States of America
(&#8220;US GAAP&#8221;) and pursuant to the rules and regulations of the SEC. These unaudited financial statements should be read in
conjunction with the audited financial statements included in the Annual Report on Form 10-K for the year ended December 31, 2025 filed
by the Company with the SEC on March 13, 2026&#160;&#160;.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000424" name="BAYA:EmergingGrowthCompanyPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_841_ecustom--EmergingGrowthCompanyPolicyTextBlock_zK4ojNLZnxy6" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><i><span id="xdx_86B_zmMhVrHqAfPi">Emerging
Growth Company</span></i></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company is an &#8220;emerging growth company,&#8221; as defined in Section 2(a) of the Securities Act of 1933, as amended (the &#8220;Securities
Act&#8221;), as modified by the Jumpstart Our Business Startups Act of 2012, as amended (the &#8220;JOBS Act&#8221;), and it may take
advantage of certain exemptions from various reporting requirements that are applicable to other public companies that are not emerging
growth companies including, but not limited to, not being required to comply with the independent registered public accounting firm attestation
requirements of Section 404 of the Sarbanes-Oxley Act, reduced disclosure obligations regarding executive compensation in its periodic
reports and proxy statements, and exemptions from the requirements of holding a nonbinding advisory vote on executive compensation and
shareholder approval of any golden parachute payments not previously approved.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Further,
Section 102(b)(1) of the JOBS Act exempts emerging growth companies from being required to comply with new or revised financial accounting
standards until private companies (that is, those that have not had a Securities Act registration statement declared effective or do
not have a class of securities registered under the Exchange Act) are required to comply with the new or revised financial accounting
standards. The JOBS Act provides that a company can elect to opt out of the extended transition period and comply with the requirements
that apply to non-emerging growth companies but any such election to opt out is irrevocable. The Company has elected not to opt out of
such extended transition period which means that when a standard is issued or revised and it has different application dates for public
or private companies, the Company, as an emerging growth company, can adopt the new or revised standard at the time private companies
adopt the new or revised standard. This may make comparison of the Company&#8217;s financial statements with another public company which
is neither an emerging growth company nor an emerging growth company which has opted out of using the extended transition period difficult
or impossible because of the potential differences in accounting standards used.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000426" name="us-gaap:UseOfEstimates"><p id="xdx_844_eus-gaap--UseOfEstimates_z1Tm1dUuHVJf" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><i><span id="xdx_865_zMoRurZQ8Wm2">Use
of Estimates</span></i></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
preparation of the financial statement in conformity with US GAAP requires the Company&#8217;s management to make estimates and assumptions
that affect the reported amounts of assets and liabilities and disclosure of contingent assets and liabilities at the date of the financial
statement.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Making
estimates requires management to exercise significant judgment. It is at least reasonably possible that the estimate of the effect of
a condition, situation or set of circumstances that existed at the date of the financial statement, which management considered in formulating
its estimate, could change in the near term due to one or more future confirming events. Accordingly, the actual results could differ
significantly from those estimates.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

Company considers all short-term investments with an original maturity of three months or less when purchased to be cash equivalents.
The Company had a cash and cash equivalent balance of $<span id="xdx_907_eus-gaap--Cash_iI_c20260331_zpMXW1KCwQDk" title="Cash"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:Cash" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000430" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">50,187</ix:nonFraction></span> and $<span id="xdx_908_eus-gaap--Cash_iI_c20251231_zMnskLZ1ed4j" title="Cash"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:Cash" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31" id="Fact000432" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">44,129</ix:nonFraction></span> as of March 31, 2026 and December 31, 2025, respectively.</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000434" name="BAYA:InvestmentHeldInTrustAccountPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_840_ecustom--InvestmentHeldInTrustAccountPolicyTextBlock_zU2TZLY0XyVl" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><i><span id="xdx_86F_zfMYvgJifIck">Investments

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company&#8217;s portfolio of investments held in the trust account is comprised of investments in U.S. government securities with a maturity
of 185 days or less or in money market funds meeting certain conditions under Rule 2a-7 under the Investment Company Act which invest
only in direct U.S. government treasury obligations and Money Market Fund. The Company&#8217;s investments held in the trust account

held in the trust account is determined using available market information. As of March 31, 2026 and December 31, 2025, the Trust Account
had balances of $<span id="xdx_906_eus-gaap--AssetsHeldInTrustNoncurrent_iI_c20260331_zLHjeSQPOhvh"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AssetsHeldInTrustNoncurrent" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000435" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">12,014,746</ix:nonFraction></span></span> <span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">and
$<span id="xdx_907_eus-gaap--AssetsHeldInTrustNoncurrent_iI_c20251231_zm7GfZcAmRxf"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AssetsHeldInTrustNoncurrent" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31" id="Fact000436" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">11,710,990</ix:nonFraction></span></span><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;,
respectively. The interests earned from the trust account totaled $<span id="xdx_90D_eus-gaap--InvestmentIncomeNet_c20260101__20260331_zo1uch63LkI7"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:InvestmentIncomeNet" contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" id="Fact000437" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">103,756</ix:nonFraction></span></span>
<span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">and $<span id="xdx_90B_eus-gaap--InvestmentIncomeNet_c20250101__20250331_z8xt89uTElIh"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:InvestmentIncomeNet" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-03-31" id="Fact000438" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">413,357</ix:nonFraction></span></span>
<span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">for the three months ended March 31, 2026 and for the three

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000440" name="BAYA:CashHeldInEscrowAccountPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_84A_ecustom--CashHeldInEscrowAccountPolicyTextBlock_znpN6jIm2Tre" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; padding: 0pt; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><i><span id="xdx_869_z6kjxRYrAtCi">Cash
held in escrow account</span></i></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; padding: 0pt; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">As
of March 31, 2026 and December 31, 2025, the Company had $<span id="xdx_901_ecustom--InvestmentHeldInEscrowAccountNonCurrent_iI_dxL_c20260331_zFws35YKcshe" title="Cash held in escrow::XDX::-"><span style="-sec-ix-hidden: xdx2ixbrl0442">0</span></span> and $<span id="xdx_904_ecustom--InvestmentHeldInEscrowAccountNonCurrent_iI_c20251231_zb9BfSJECPsl" title="Cash held in escrow"><ix:nonFraction name="BAYA:InvestmentHeldInEscrowAccountNonCurrent" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31" id="Fact000444" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">50,000</ix:nonFraction></span>, respectively, of cash held in escrow, representing the Extension
Payment that was subsequently deposited into the Trust Account in the month following the balance sheet date. The $<span id="xdx_90B_ecustom--ExtensionPayment_c20250101__20251231_zCM4JR0Wb6u1" title="Extension payment"><ix:nonFraction name="BAYA:ExtensionPayment" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-12-31" id="Fact000446" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">50,000</ix:nonFraction></span> balance at
December 31, 2025 represented an Extension Payment that had not yet been deposited into the Trust Account; the full amount was subsequently
deposited into the Trust Account on January 7, 2026.</span></p>
<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000448" name="BAYA:OfferingCostsPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_849_ecustom--OfferingCostsPolicyTextBlock_zIaGJBMM5nAj" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><i><span id="xdx_86C_zeLDTFDRBW8i">Offering
Costs</span></i></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Offering
costs consist of legal, accounting, and other costs (including underwriting discounts and commissions) incurred through the balance sheet
date that are directly related to the IPO and that were charged to shareholders&#8217; equity upon the completion of the IPO on December
19, 2023.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000450" name="us-gaap:IncomeTaxPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_84D_eus-gaap--IncomeTaxPolicyTextBlock_znmfawswb3Lj" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><i><span id="xdx_867_zGq0D1xOr7Ai">Income
Taxes</span></i></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: center"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company follows the asset and liability method of accounting for income taxes under ASC 740, &#8220;<i>Income Taxes</i>.&#8221; Deferred
tax assets and liabilities are recognized for the estimated future tax consequences attributable to differences between the financial
statements carrying amounts of existing assets and liabilities and their respective tax bases. Deferred tax assets and liabilities are
measured using enacted tax rates expected to apply to taxable income in the years in which those temporary differences are expected to
be recovered or settled. The effect on deferred tax assets and liabilities of a change in tax rates is recognized in income in the period
that included the enactment date. Valuation allowances are established, when necessary, to reduce deferred tax assets to the amount expected
to be realized.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">ASC
740 prescribes a recognition threshold and a measurement attribute for the financial statement recognition and measurement of tax positions
taken or expected to be taken in a tax return. For those benefits to be recognized, a tax position must be more likely than not to be
sustained upon examination by taxing authorities. The Company recognizes accrued interest and penalties related to unrecognized tax benefits
as income tax expense. There were <span id="xdx_90D_eus-gaap--UnrecognizedTaxBenefits_iI_do_c20260331_zWyCuGoCbGy9" title="Unrecognized tax benefits"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:UnrecognizedTaxBenefits" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000452" format="ixt-sec:numwordsen" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">no</ix:nonFraction></span> unrecognized tax benefits and <span id="xdx_909_eus-gaap--IncomeTaxExaminationPenaltiesAndInterestAccrued_iI_do_c20260331_zRZu29K0Uk17" title="Accrued interest and penalties"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:IncomeTaxExaminationPenaltiesAndInterestAccrued" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000454" format="ixt-sec:numwordsen" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">no</ix:nonFraction></span> amounts accrued for interest and penalties as of March 31, 2026.
The Company is currently not aware of any issues under review that could result in significant payments, accruals, or material deviation
from its position.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">There
is currently no taxation imposed on income by the Government of the Cayman Islands. In accordance with Cayman income tax regulations,
income taxes are not levied on the Company. Consequently, income taxes are not reflected in the Company&#8217;s financial statements.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000456" name="us-gaap:EarningsPerSharePolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_840_eus-gaap--EarningsPerSharePolicyTextBlock_zI7b87ytDsPj" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><i><span id="xdx_86A_zIlLjbQUVCS6">Net
Income (Loss) per Ordinary Share</span></i></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company complies with accounting and disclosure requirements of FASB ASC 260, Earnings Per Share. The statements of operations include
a presentation of income (loss) per redeemable share and income (loss) per non-redeemable share following the two-class method of income
per share. In order to determine the net income (loss) attributable to both the redeemable shares and non-redeemable shares, the Company
first considered the undistributed income (loss) allocable to both the redeemable shares and non-redeemable shares and the undistributed
income (loss) is calculated using the total net loss less any dividends paid. The Company then allocated the undistributed income (loss)
ratably based on the weighted average number of shares outstanding between the redeemable and non-redeemable shares. Any remeasurement
of the accretion to redemption value of the common shares subject to possible redemption was considered to be dividends paid to the public
shareholders. As of March 31, 2026, the Company did not have any dilutive securities and other contracts that could, potentially, be
exercised or converted into ordinary shares and then share in the earnings of the Company. As a result, diluted income (loss) per share
is the same as basic income (loss) per share for the period presented.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt"></p><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000472" name="us-gaap:ScheduleOfEarningsPerShareBasicAndDilutedTableTextBlock"><p id="xdx_892_eus-gaap--ScheduleOfEarningsPerShareBasicAndDilutedTableTextBlock_zLxaEBe1Xpak" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><span id="xdx_8B9_zBm627x28QTj" style="display: none">SCHEDULE OF EARNINGS PER SHARE BASIC AND DILUTED</span></span></p>

<table cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%">
  <tr style="display: none; font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; vertical-align: bottom">

  </table>

</ix:nonNumeric><p id="xdx_8A9_zRj33xixTvyc" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000526" name="us-gaap:ConcentrationRiskCreditRisk"><p id="xdx_848_eus-gaap--ConcentrationRiskCreditRisk_zuy8WO0XzgP7" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><i><span id="xdx_86B_zrk1wNgVe9e4">Concentration
of Credit Risk</span></i></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: center; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Financial
instruments that potentially subject the Company to concentrations of credit risk consist of cash accounts in a financial institution,
which, at times, may exceed the Federal Depository Insurance Coverage of $<span id="xdx_90B_eus-gaap--CashFDICInsuredAmount_iI_c20260331_zgG3Zl6FKF5c" title="FDIC insured amount"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:CashFDICInsuredAmount" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000528" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">250,000</ix:nonFraction></span>. The Company has not experienced losses on these accounts
and management believes the Company is not exposed to significant risks on such accounts.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000530" name="us-gaap:FairValueOfFinancialInstrumentsPolicy"><p id="xdx_84E_eus-gaap--FairValueOfFinancialInstrumentsPolicy_zZ4FdkVUBmHa" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><i><span id="xdx_863_zgiCaGJrWV26">Fair
Value of Financial Instruments</span></i></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
fair value of the Company&#8217;s assets and liabilities, which qualify as financial instruments under ASC 820, &#8220;<i>Fair Value
Measurement</i>,&#8221; approximates the carrying amounts represented in the balance sheet, primarily due to their short-term nature.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000532" name="us-gaap:SharesSubjectToMandatoryRedemptionChangesInRedemptionValuePolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_848_eus-gaap--SharesSubjectToMandatoryRedemptionChangesInRedemptionValuePolicyTextBlock_zPjOIg7aMSFd" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><i><span id="xdx_860_zJWE8dJSEBhb">Ordinary
Shares Subject to Possible Redemption</span></i></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company accounts for its ordinary shares subject to possible redemption in accordance with the guidance in Accounting Standards Codification
(&#8220;ASC&#8221;) Topic 480 &#8220;Distinguishing Liabilities from Equity.&#8221; Ordinary shares subject to mandatory redemption (if
any) is classified as a liability instrument and is measured at fair value. Conditionally redeemable ordinary shares (including ordinary
shares that features redemption rights that are either within the control of the holder or subject to redemption upon the occurrence
of uncertain events not solely within the Company&#8217;s control) is classified as temporary equity. At all other times, ordinary shares
are classified as shareholders&#8217; equity. The Company&#8217;s ordinary shares features certain redemption rights that are considered
to be outside of the Company&#8217;s control and subject to occurrence of uncertain future events. Accordingly, ordinary shares subject
to possible redemption are presented at redemption value as temporary equity, outside of the shareholders&#8217; equity section of the
Company&#8217;s balance sheet. The Company recognizes changes in redemption value immediately as they occur and adjusts the carrying
value of redeemable ordinary shares to equal the redemption value at the end of each reporting period. Increases or decreases in the
carrying amount of redeemable ordinary shares are affected by charges against additional paid in capital and accumulated deficit.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000594" name="BAYA:InitialPublicOfferingTextBlock"><p id="xdx_802_ecustom--InitialPublicOfferingTextBlock_zooBKArk43ab" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>NOTE
3 &#8212; <span id="xdx_826_z8y6FOJ0CQ2i">INITIAL PUBLIC OFFERING</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">On
December 19, 2023, the Company sold <span id="xdx_905_eus-gaap--StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues_pid_c20231219__20231219__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--IPOMember_zwHt0dYiLgI7" title="Stock issued shares new issues"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues" contextRef="From2023-12-192023-12-19_us-gaap_IPOMember" id="Fact000596" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">6,000,000</ix:nonFraction></span> Units at a price of $<span id="xdx_900_eus-gaap--SaleOfStockPricePerShare_iI_pid_c20231219__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--IPOMember_zIiDSGIHW0Rl" title="Sale of stock, par share"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SaleOfStockPricePerShare" contextRef="AsOf2023-12-19_us-gaap_IPOMember" id="Fact000598" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="USDPShares">10.00</ix:nonFraction></span> per Unit. <span id="xdx_903_eus-gaap--SaleOfStockDescriptionOfTransaction_c20231219__20231219_z3ShgTeguk03" title="Stock holders equity related to private placement description"><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2023-12-192023-12-19" id="Fact000600" name="us-gaap:SaleOfStockDescriptionOfTransaction">Each Unit consists of one ordinary share and one right
to receive one-tenth (1/10) of one ordinary share upon the consummation of the Company&#8217;s initial Business Combination. Ten Public
Rights will entitle the holder to one ordinary share (see Note 7). The Company will not issue fractional shares and only whole shares
will trade, so unless a holder purchased units in multiples of tens, such holder will not be able to receive or trade the fractional
shares underlying the rights.</ix:nonNumeric></span></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000602" name="BAYA:PrivatePlacementsTextBlock"><p id="xdx_807_ecustom--PrivatePlacementsTextBlock_zpCr7FzV5Feg" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>NOTE
4 &#8212; <span id="xdx_822_zZD0cmFZWdxk">PRIVATE PLACEMENTS</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Simultaneously
with the closing of the IPO, the Company consummated the private sale of <span id="xdx_908_eus-gaap--StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues_c20231219__20231219__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--PrivatePlacementMember_zxkJHfVd2Odg" title="Stock issued shares new issues"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues" contextRef="From2023-12-192023-12-19_us-gaap_PrivatePlacementMember" id="Fact000604" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">232,500</ix:nonFraction></span> Private Placement Units at a price of $<span id="xdx_90C_eus-gaap--SharesIssuedPricePerShare_iI_pid_c20231219__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--PrivatePlacementMember_z8iNBDjGZmzj" title="Share price"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SharesIssuedPricePerShare" contextRef="AsOf2023-12-19_us-gaap_PrivatePlacementMember" id="Fact000606" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="USDPShares">10.00</ix:nonFraction></span> per Private
Placement Unit, generating gross proceeds of $<span id="xdx_909_eus-gaap--ProceedsFromIssuanceOfPrivatePlacement_c20231219__20231219__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--PrivatePlacementMember_zayoXGbaj6Vd" title="Proceeds from private placement"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ProceedsFromIssuanceOfPrivatePlacement" contextRef="From2023-12-192023-12-19_us-gaap_PrivatePlacementMember" id="Fact000608" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">2,325,000</ix:nonFraction></span>. <span id="xdx_90B_eus-gaap--SaleOfStockDescriptionOfTransaction_c20231219__20231219__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--PrivatePlacementMember_zmpXCze1ci92" title="Stock holders equity related to private placement description"><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2023-12-192023-12-19_us-gaap_PrivatePlacementMember" id="Fact000610" name="us-gaap:SaleOfStockDescriptionOfTransaction">Each Private Placement Unit consists of one ordinary share and one right to
receive one-tenth (1/10) of one ordinary share upon the consummation of the Company&#8217;s initial Business Combination.</ix:nonNumeric></span> The proceeds
from the sale of the Private Placement Units were added to the net proceeds from the IPO held in the Trust Account. If the Company does
not complete a Business Combination within the Combination Period, the proceeds from the sale of the Private Placement Units held in
the Trust Account will be used to fund the redemption of the Public Shares (subject to the requirements of applicable law). The Private
Placement Units (including the underlying securities) will not be transferable, assignable, or salable until the completion of a Business
Combination, subject to certain exceptions.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">On
February 23, 2023, our sponsor, Bayview Holding LP, acquired <span id="xdx_909_eus-gaap--StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues_c20230223__20230223__srt--TitleOfIndividualAxis__custom--SponsorsMember_zCpoBUbNFZcb" title="Stock issued shares new issues"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues" contextRef="From2023-02-232023-02-23_custom_SponsorsMember" id="Fact000614" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">1,437,500</ix:nonFraction></span> founder shares for an aggregate price of $<span id="xdx_908_eus-gaap--StockIssuedDuringPeriodValueNewIssues_c20230223__20230223__srt--TitleOfIndividualAxis__custom--SponsorsMember_zQST7HMmsIig" title="Stock issued value new issues"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodValueNewIssues" contextRef="From2023-02-232023-02-23_custom_SponsorsMember" id="Fact000616" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">25,000</ix:nonFraction></span>. <span id="xdx_908_eus-gaap--StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues_c20230314__20230314__us-gaap--StatementClassOfStockAxis__custom--FounderSharesMember__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--PeaceInvestmentHoldingsLimitedMember_z2XMR21MhX45" title="Shares issued"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues" contextRef="From2023-03-142023-03-14_custom_FounderSharesMember_custom_PeaceInvestmentHoldingsLimitedMember" id="Fact000618" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">963,125</ix:nonFraction></span> founder
shares were transferred to our sponsor Peace Investment Holdings Limited on March 14, 2023.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">On
December 14, 2023, the Company issued <span id="xdx_906_eus-gaap--StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues_c20231214__20231214__us-gaap--StatementClassOfStockAxis__custom--FounderSharesMember_zVnmmbpNbgA4" title="Stock issued shares new issues"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues" contextRef="From2023-12-142023-12-14_custom_FounderSharesMember" id="Fact000620" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">287,500</ix:nonFraction></span> founder shares for a consideration of $<span id="xdx_90F_eus-gaap--StockIssuedDuringPeriodValueNewIssues_c20231214__20231214__us-gaap--StatementClassOfStockAxis__custom--FounderSharesMember_zIdFyvEsCf19" title="Stock issued value new issues"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodValueNewIssues" contextRef="From2023-12-142023-12-14_custom_FounderSharesMember" id="Fact000622" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">100</ix:nonFraction></span>, resulting in Bayview Holding LP holding a total
of <span id="xdx_90F_eus-gaap--StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues_c20231214__20231214__srt--TitleOfIndividualAxis__custom--SponsorsMember__us-gaap--StatementClassOfStockAxis__custom--FounderSharesMember_zF5o5UBJc4Dh" title="Stock issued shares new issues"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues" contextRef="From2023-12-142023-12-14_custom_SponsorsMember_custom_FounderSharesMember" id="Fact000624" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">569,250</ix:nonFraction></span> founder shares and Peace Investment Holdings Limited holding a total of <span id="xdx_901_eus-gaap--StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues_c20231214__20231214__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--PeaceInvestmentHoldingsLimitedMember__us-gaap--StatementClassOfStockAxis__custom--FounderSharesMember_zGASFGmZHpN4" title="Number of shares issued"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues" contextRef="From2023-12-142023-12-14_custom_PeaceInvestmentHoldingsLimitedMember_custom_FounderSharesMember" id="Fact000626" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">1,155,750</ix:nonFraction></span> founder shares. 6 &#8212; <span id="xdx_829_zC0k8b04VsF7">COMMITMENTS AND CONTINGENCIES</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><i>Registration
Rights</i></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
holders of the Founder Shares, Private Placement Units, securities underlying the unit purchase option (&#8220;UPO&#8221;), and Units
that may be issued upon conversion of working capital loans (and all underlying securities) will be entitled to registration rights pursuant
to a registration rights agreement requiring the Company to register such securities for resale. Subject to certain limitations set forth
in such agreement, the holders of these securities will be entitled to make up to three demands, excluding short form registration demands,
that the Company register such securities. In addition, the holders have certain &#8220;piggy-back&#8221; registration rights with respect
to registration statements filed subsequent to completion of a Business Combination and rights to require the Company to register for
resale such securities pursuant to Rule 415 under the Securities Act. However, the registration rights agreement provides that the Company
will not be required to effect or permit any registration or cause any registration statement to become effective until the securities
covered thereby are released from their lock-up restrictions. The Company will bear the expenses incurred in connection with the filing
of any such registration statements.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The

additional Units to cover over-allotment, at the IPO price less the underwriting discounts and commissions. On January 28, 2024, the
underwriter&#8217;s over-allotment option expired unexercised and hence a total of <span id="xdx_90C_eus-gaap--ShareBasedCompensationArrangementByShareBasedPaymentAwardOptionsForfeituresInPeriod_c20240128__20240128__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--OverAllotmentOptionMember_z8kRTD2wqlli" title="Number of shares forfeited by sponsors"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ShareBasedCompensationArrangementByShareBasedPaymentAwardOptionsForfeituresInPeriod" contextRef="From2024-01-282024-01-28_us-gaap_OverAllotmentOptionMember" id="Fact000650" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">225,000</ix:nonFraction></span>
ordinary shares were forfeited by the Sponsors.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt; padding: 0pt; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The

if the underwriter&#8217;s over-allotment option is exercised in full), payable upon the closing of the IPO. The cash underwriting discount
of $<span id="xdx_90A_ecustom--CashUnderwritingDiscount_c20231219__20231219_zBTxPmfUFNQ1" title="Cash underwriting discount"><ix:nonFraction name="BAYA:CashUnderwritingDiscount" contextRef="From2023-12-192023-12-19" id="Fact000658" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">1,200,000</ix:nonFraction></span> was paid on December 19, 2023.</span></p>
